            cmd_LNG_cut_out = 0;

            cmd_DG1_cut_out = 0;
            cmd_DG2_cut_in = 1;
            cmd_DG2_cut_out = 0;
            cmd_load_cut_in = 0;
            cmd_load_cut_out = 1;
        }
        exit {
            illegal_overload_state = 0;
        }
    }
}

        }

        }

            if [SoC >= 0.2 && PL - Ppv - Pw - Pgmax - Pd1max - eng3_Pmax <= Pbatmax] {

            cmd_DG1_cut_out = 0;
            cmd_DG2_cut_in = 1;
            cmd_DG2_cut_out = 0;
            cmd_load_cut_in = 0;
            cmd_load_cut_out = 1;
        }

            illegal_overload_state = 0;
        }
    }

    ! * -> AllThermalBatteryLack : if [PL > 0 && Ppv + Pw < PL && PL - Ppv - Pw > Pgmax + Pd1max + eng3_Pmax];

    [*] -> ZeroLoadCharge;
